Observed listing — 2026-10-24

Weekend note: Saturday is not a federal business day. Most federal publishers issue few or no documents on weekends and federal holidays; this stream may stay short until the next business day.

    Jesus Diaz v. Attorney General United States of America (opens in a new tab)
    Judicial opinion · govinfo API · USCOURTS-ca3-25-02190 / USCOURTS-ca3-25-02190-0

    FAPD-AI: The Third Circuit affirmed the Board of Immigration Appeals' denial of asylum, withholding of removal, and Convention Against Torture protection for a Mexican national removable based on two criminal convictions involving moral turpitude. The court found the applicant failed to establish past persecution based on a single threat without physical harm, did not meet the withholding of removal standard, and did not demonstrate he would more likely than not face torture if returned to Mexico.     FAPD-AI: The court of appeals denied Jones' request for a certificate of appealability regarding his challenge to the district court's dismissal of his first two arguments as unauthorized second motions, finding that the district court correctly determined such arguments must proceed under 28 U.S.C. § 2255, not § 3582(c) compassionate release, per the Supreme Court's decision in Fernandez. The court affirmed the denial of compassionate release based on sentencing disparities, finding they do not meet the statutory requirements for such relief. USCOURTS-SEL-01

    Newsom, Et AL. V. Trump, Et AL. (opens in a new tab)
    Judicial opinion · govinfo API · USCOURTS-ca9-25-3727 / USCOURTS-ca9-25-3727-0

    FAPD-AI: The Ninth Circuit Court of Appeals affirmed a preliminary injunction preventing the President from deploying members of the California National Guard in Los Angeles, finding that the President's order federalizing the Guard under 10 U.S.C. § 12406 lacked statutory authority and violated the constitutional separation of powers. The panel concluded the President did not make required threshold showings under either subsection 12406(3) or 12406(2). USCOURTS-SEL-01

      USA v. Ashley Grayson (opens in a new tab)
      Judicial opinion · govinfo API · USCOURTS-ca6-24-05988 / USCOURTS-ca6-24-05988-0

      FAPD-AI: The Sixth Circuit Court of Appeals affirmed Ashley Grayson's conviction for conspiracy to use interstate commerce facilities in the commission of murder-for-hire in violation of 18 U.S.C. § 1958. Grayson had hired Olivia Johnson and Brandon Thomas to kill three people, and Johnson recorded a FaceTime call in which Grayson discussed the murders before providing the recording to federal authorities. The court rejected Grayson's challenges to the recording's admissibility and jury instructions. USCOURTS-SEL-01

    Judicial opinion · govinfo API · USCOURTS-ca11-26-10485 / USCOURTS-ca11-26-10485-0

    FAPD-AI: Bay Haven sought additional insurance recovery from Hartford for flood damage from Hurricane Ian beyond amounts already paid, but Hartford refused without new signed and sworn proofs of loss as required by the National Flood Insurance Program standard policy.     Judicial opinion · govinfo API · USCOURTS-ca13-26-01602 / USCOURTS-ca13-26-01602-0

    FAPD-AI: The Federal Circuit affirmed the Merit Systems Protection Board's decision sustaining the removal of an Immigration Services Officer II from the Department of Homeland Security for lack of candor and unauthorized database searches. The Board's penalty determination was supported by substantial evidence and did not constitute an abuse of discretion. USCOURTS-SEL-01
